   - Node A is connected to nodes P and S.
   - Node I is connected to nodes P, Q, H, and N.
3. **Identify the paths from A to I:**
   - Path 1: A -> P -> I
   - Path 2: A -> S -> E -> B -> T -> N -> H -> I
   - Path 3: A -> S -> E -> B -> T -> N -> I
   - Path 4: A -> S -> E -> B -> T -> C -> H -> I
   - Path 5: A -> S -> E -> B -> F -> C -> H -> I
   - Path 6: A -> S -> E -> B -> F -> Q -> I
   - Path 7: A -> S -> E-> B -> C -> N -> H -> I
   - Path 8: A -> S -> E -> B -> F -> R -> J -> Q -> I
   
4. **Identify the bottlenecks in these paths:**
   - Path 1 can be cut by removing P.
   - Path 2, Path 3, Path 4, Path 7 can be cut by removing B.
   - Path 5, Path 6, Path 8 can also be cut by removing E.

   Removing nodes P, B, or E would seem sufficient, as there will be no remaining path between A and I if any one of these nodes are removed.

   However, we need to minimize the number of nodes. Notice that if we remove P and B, it completely disconnects paths A -> P -> I, and all paths via S -> E -> B.

   If we just remove B, we still might have paths via P and Q, so we must remove P and B together as our bottle neck nodes to completely disconnect.

**Answer:**

6. **Check the solution:**

- **Direct Connections from H**:
  - H is connected to N.
  - H is connected to I.

- **From N**:
  - N is connected to T, which is connected to B, which is connected to Q.
  - N is connected to O, which is connected to G, which is not connected to Q.
  - N is connected to C, which is connected to B, which is connected to Q.

- **From I**:
  - I is connected to A, which is connected to P, which is connected to Q.
  - I is connected to S, which is connected to E, which is connected to B, which is connected to Q.
  - I is connected to J, which is connected to Q.

### Determining the Cut Set

To disconnect H and Q, we need to remove the edges that are part of all paths connecting these nodes. The key edges to consider are:
- (H, N)
- (H, I)
- (N, T) and (T, B) or (N, C) and (C, B) or (N, O) and (O, G)
- (I, A) and (A, P) or (I, S) and (S, E) and (E, B) or (I, J)

The minimal set of edges that need to be removed are:
- (H, N) to disconnect the path through N.
- (H, I) to disconnect the path through I.
- (Q, I) to break the direct connection through I.

### Answer
